2025-06-17T02:52:53.4343520Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4343637Z ## Tailscale OAuth Client Setup Instructions
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4343843Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4343928Z ### 1. Create OAuth Client
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4344428Z 1. Go to the [OAuth clients page](https://login.tailscale.com/admin/settings/oauth) in your Tailscale admin console
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4344977Z 2. Click **Generate OAuth client**
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4345445Z 3. Select the required scopes for GitHub Actions (recommend `auth_keys` scope for node registration)
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4346013Z 4. Add the `tag:ci` tag (or create a custom tag for GitHub Actions)
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4346362Z 5. Click **Generate client**
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4346851Z 6. **Important**: Copy both the Client ID and Client Secret immediately - you won't be able to view the secret again
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4347285Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4347381Z ### 2. Add GitHub Secrets
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4347667Z Add these to your repository's GitHub Actions secrets:
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4348016Z - `TS_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`: Your OAuth client ID
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4348329Z - `TS_OAUTH_SECRET`: Your OAuth client secret  
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4348552Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4348646Z ### 3. Repository Configuration
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4349769Z The `.github/workflows/copilot-setup-steps.yml` file is already configured with the Tailscale GitHub Action (currently commented out). Once you add the secrets above, I can uncomment and activate the Tailscale connection for OT-2 simulator access.
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4350957Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4351164Z ### 4. Verification
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4351873Z After setup, the workflow will automatically connect to your Tailscale network using the `tag:ci` tag, allowing secure access to your OT-2 simulator for testing Prefect installations.
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4352548Z

2025-06-17T02:52:53.4357667Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4357943Z Based on the Tailscale documentation, here's how to update your ACLs to add the new `tag:ci` tag:
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4358330Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4358414Z ## ACL JSON Configuration
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4358564Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4358783Z Add the tag to your `tagOwners` section in the Tailscale admin console ACL editor:
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4359116Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4359197Z ```json
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4359367Z {
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4359536Z   "tagOwners": {
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4359753Z     "tag:ci": ["your-email@domain.com"]
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4360132Z   },
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4360306Z   "acls": [
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4360479Z     {
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4360660Z       "action": "accept",
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4360884Z       "src": ["tag:ci"],
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4361106Z       "dst": ["*:*"]
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4361303Z     }
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4361468Z   ]
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4361631Z }
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4361793Z ```
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4361896Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4361980Z ## Admin Console Steps
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4362121Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4362411Z 1. Go to [Access Controls](https://login.tailscale.com/admin/acls) in your Tailscale admin console
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4362926Z 2. Add the `tag:ci` entry to your `tagOwners` section 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4363441Z 3. Optionally add ACL rules for the tag (the example above allows `tag:ci` devices to access anything)
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4363912Z 4. Save the policy file
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4364057Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4364147Z ## OAuth Client Configuration
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4364314Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4364781Z The OAuth client you created should automatically work with any tags defined in your ACL. No additional admin console configuration is needed beyond:
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4365354Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4365570Z 1. Creating the OAuth client with appropriate scopes (`auth_keys` recommended)
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4366051Z 2. Adding the `tag:ci` tag to your ACL policy as shown above
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4366492Z 3. Adding the GitHub secrets (`TS_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` and `TS_OAUTH_SECRET`)
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4366784Z 
2025-06-17T02:52:53.4367322Z The Tailscale GitHub Action will use the OAuth client to authenticate and automatically apply the `tag:ci` tag to the GitHub Actions runner when it connects to your tailnet.
